User2026-02-22T22:34:48.990Z

Implement the following plan:

Plan: Pivot to Pandoc Backend (Phase 1 — v0.2.0)

Context

Schopenhauer currently renders YAML/JSON specs to .docx only via python-docx. The CLAUDE.md defines a migration to Pandoc as the rendering engine, making Markdown the source of truth and enabling multi-format output (docx, pdf, html, pptx, epub). This pivot implements Phase 1: add pypandoc as a backend, keep legacy working via --backend legacy.

Steps

1. Create branch and restore deleted files

  • Create pivot branch from main
  • Restore .gitignore, pyproject.toml, README.md, Dockerfile from git (they're deleted in working tree)

2. Update pyproject.toml

  • Bump version to 0.2.0
  • Add pypandoc-binary>=1.15 to dependencies (keep python-docx)
  • Update requires-python to >=3.10
  • Add templates/ and schemas/ to sdist includes
  • Update keywords/classifiers for multi-format support

3. Create src/will/preprocess.py (new file)

  • preprocess(content, variables) — Jinja2-based {{var}} substitution
  • Permissive undefined handling (unresolved vars pass through for backward compat)
  • PreprocessError exception class

4. Create src/will/spec_compiler.py (new file)

  • compile_spec(spec: dict) -> str — converts legacy spec dict to Markdown+frontmatter
  • compile_spec_file(path) -> str — loads YAML/JSON file, calls compile_spec
  • Section type mapping (mirrors core.py:1036-1102):
  • heading → # Title (with level)
  • section → optional \newpage + # Title + optional *subtitle*
  • content → heading + text + - bullets or 1. numbered
  • table → pipe-delimited Markdown table
  • image → ![caption](path){ width=Xin }
  • quote → > "text"\n> --- author
  • code → fenced code block with language
  • page_break → \newpage
  • horizontal_line → ---

5. Create src/will/pandoc_backend.py (new file)

  • convert(source, output_path, output_format, reference_doc, extra_args, metadata) -> Path
  • convert_to_bytes(...) -> bytes
  • get_pandoc_version() -> str
  • get_supported_formats() -> dict
  • PandocBackendError exception class
  • Format-specific defaults (standalone, pdf-engine, self-contained)

6. Create src/will/render.py (new file)

  • render(source, output, format, template, variables, backend) -> Path | bytes
  • Input detection: .md read directly, .yaml/.json compiled via spec_compiler
  • Frontmatter extraction via regex (---\n...\n---)
  • Variable merging (frontmatter will.variables + parameter)
  • Preprocessing via preprocess()
  • Template resolution: name → templates/reference-docs/{name}.docx
  • Pandoc conversion via pandoc_backend.convert()
  • Legacy fallback: backend="legacy" delegates to WordDocument.from_spec()
  • RenderError, _extract_frontmatter(), _resolve_template(), _rebuild_markdown()

7. Update src/will/__init__.py

  • Bump __version__ to "0.2.0"
  • Add from will.render import render export
  • Update __all__ and module docstring

8. Update src/will/cli.py

  • Add will render <input> -o <output> command with options:
  • -f/--format (multiple, for multi-format output)
  • -t/--template (template name or path)
  • -V/--var (KEY=VALUE variable substitution)
  • --backend (pandoc or legacy)
  • Keep all existing commands (create, generate, inspect, etc.) unchanged

9. Create template files

  • templates/reference-docs/default.docx — generated via pypandoc
  • templates/defaults/docx.yaml, pdf.yaml, html.yaml — Pandoc defaults

10. Create examples/report.md

  • Markdown equivalent of examples/report.yaml with YAML frontmatter and {{variable}} tokens

11. Create tests

  • tests/test_preprocess.py — variable substitution, edge cases
  • tests/test_spec_compiler.py — all section types, frontmatter, file loading
  • tests/test_render.py — end-to-end: .md→.docx, .yaml→.docx, .md→.html, variables, legacy backend
  • tests/test_pandoc_backend.py — pypandoc wrapper, format conversion

12. Install dependencies and verify

  • pip install -e ".[dev]" to install with new pypandoc-binary
  • Run full test suite
  • Test CLI: will render examples/report.md -o outputs/report.docx

Verification

  1. pytest — all new and existing tests pass

    46→# CLI GROUP

    48→

    50→@click.group(invoke_without_command=True)

    53→def main(ctx, version):
    54→    """
    55→    Schopenhauer's Will - A powerful Word document generator.
    56→

    58→    or create them interactively.

    60→    \b

    63→        will generate spec.yaml -o output.docx
    64→        will template init report -o spec.yaml

    66→    """
    67→    if version:
    68→        console.print(f"[bold]Schopenhauer's Will[/bold] version [cyan]{__version__}[/cyan]")
    69→        console.print(
    70→            f"Primary color: [bold #{BRAND['primary']}]Burgundy[/bold #{BRAND['primary']}]"
    71→        )
    72→        ctx.exit()
    73→
    74→    if ctx.invoked_subcommand is None:
    75→        console.print(ctx.get_help())
    76→
    77→
    78→# =============================================================================
    79→# CREATE COMMAND
    80→# =============================================================================
    81→
    82→
    83→@main.command()
    84→@click.option("--output", "-o", required=True, help="Output file path (.docx)")
    85→@click.option("--title", "-t", help="Document title")
    86→@click.option("--subtitle", "-s", help="Document subtitle")
    87→@click.option("--author", "-a", help="Document author")
    88→@click.option("--template", help="Template file (.docx) or built-in template name")
    89→@click.option(
    90→    "--page-size",
    91→    type=click.Choice(["letter", "legal", "a4", "a5"]),
    92→    default="letter",
    93→    help="Page size",
    94→)
    95→@click.option(
    96→    "--margins",
    97→    type=click.Choice(["normal", "narrow", "moderate", "wide"]),
    98→    default="normal",
    99→    help="Page margins",
   100→)
   101→@click.option("--header", help="Header text")
   102→@click.option("--footer", help="Footer text")
   103→@click.option(
   104→    "--page-numbers/--no-page-numbers", default=True, help="Include page numbers in footer"
   105→)
   106→def create(
   107→    output: str,
   108→    title: Optional[str],
   109→    subtitle: Optional[str],
   110→    author: Optional[str],
   111→    template: Optional[str],
   112→    page_size: str,
   113→    margins: str,
   114→    header: Optional[str],
   115→    footer: Optional[str],
   116→    page_numbers: bool,
   117→):
   118→    """
   119→    Create a new Word document.
   120→
   121→    \b
   122→    Examples:
   123→        will create -o report.docx --title "My Report"
   124→        will create -o letter.docx --template letter --title "Business Letter"
   125→        will create -o doc.docx -t "Title" -a "Author" --page-size a4
   126→    """
   127→    try:
   128→        # Check if template is a file path or built-in name
   129→        template_path = None
   130→        if template:
   131→            if Path(template).exists():
   132→                template_path = template
   133→            elif template not in get_template_names():
   134→                console.print(
   135→                    f"[yellow]Warning: Template '{template}' not found, using default[/yellow]"
   136→                )
   137→
   138→        doc = WordDocument(template=template_path, title=title, author=author)
   139→
   140→        # Apply page settings
   141→        doc.set_page_size(preset=page_size)
   142→        doc.set_margins(preset=margins)
   143→
   144→        # Apply built-in template if specified
   145→        if template and template in get_template_names():
   146→            from will.templates import apply_template
   147→
   148→            apply_template(doc, template)
   149→
   150→        # Add title
   151→        if title:
   152→            doc.add_title(title, subtitle)
   153→            doc.add_page_break()
   154→
   155→        # Set header/footer
   156→        if header:
   157→            doc.set_header(header)
   158→
   159→        doc.set_footer(footer or "", include_page_numbers=page_numbers)
   160→
   161→        # Save
   162→        doc.save(output)
   163→
   164→        console.print(f"[green]Created document:[/green] {output}")
   165→
   166→        # Show document info
   167→        info = doc.get_info()
   168→        table = Table(show_header=False, box=None)
   169→        table.add_column("Property", style="dim")
   170→        table.add_column("Value")
   171→        table.add_row("Title", info["title"] or "(none)")
   172→        table.add_row("Author", info["author"] or "(none)")
   173→        table.add_row("Page Size", page_size)
   174→        table.add_row("Margins", margins)
   175→        console.print(table)
   176→
   177→    except Exception as e:
   178→        console.print(f"[red]Error creating document:[/red] {e}")
   179→        sys.exit(1)
   180→
   181→
   182→# =============================================================================
   183→# GENERATE COMMAND
   184→# =============================================================================
   185→
   186→
   187→@main.command()
   188→@click.argument("spec_file", type=click.Path(exists=True))
   189→@click.option("--output", "-o", required=True, help="Output file path (.docx)")
   190→@click.option("--template", "-t", help="Optional template file (.docx)")
   191→@click.option("--var", "-V", multiple=True, help="Variable substitution (KEY=VALUE)")
   192→def generate(
   193→    spec_file: str,
   194→    output: str,
   195→    template: Optional[str],
   196→    var: tuple,
   197→):
   198→    """
   199→    Generate a Word document from a YAML or JSON specification.
   200→
   201→    \b
   202→    Examples:
   203→        will generate report.yaml -o report.docx
   204→        will generate spec.json -o doc.docx --template base.docx
   205→        will generate spec.yaml -o doc.docx -V AUTHOR="John" -V DATE="2024"
   206→    """
   207→    try:
   208→        spec_path = Path(spec_file)
   209→
   210→        click.echo("Generating document...")
   211→
   212→        # Load spec
   213→        if spec_path.suffix.lower() in [".yaml", ".yml"]:
   214→            import yaml
   215→
   216→            with open(spec_path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
   217→                spec = yaml.safe_load(f)
   218→        else:
   219→            with open(spec_path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
   220→                spec = json.load(f)
   221→
   222→        # Apply template override
   223→        if template:
   224→            spec["template"] = template
   225→
   226→        # Create document from spec
   227→        doc = WordDocument.from_spec(spec)
   228→
   229→        # Apply variable substitutions
   230→        if var:
   231→            replacements = {}
   232→            for v in var:
   233→                if "=" in v:
   234→                    key, value = v.split("=", 1)
   235→                    replacements[key] = value
   236→            if replacements:
   237→                doc.replace_placeholders(replacements)
   238→
   239→        # Save
   240→        doc.save(output)
   241→
   242→        click.echo(click.style(f"Generated document: {output}", fg="green"))
   243→
   244→        # Show summary
   245→        info = doc.get_info()
   246→        click.echo(f"  Paragraphs: {info['paragraphs']}")
   247→        click.echo(f"  Tables: {info['tables']}")
   248→        click.echo(f"  Word count: {info['word_count']}")
   249→
   250→    except Exception as e:
   251→        click.echo(click.style(f"Error generating document: {e}", fg="red"), err=True)
   252→        sys.exit(1)
   253→
   254→
   255→# =============================================================================
   256→# INSPECT COMMAND
   257→# =============================================================================
   258→
   259→
   260→@main.command()
   261→@click.argument("document", type=click.Path(exists=True))
   262→@click.option("--json", "as_json", is_flag=True, help="Output as JSON")
   263→@click.option("--placeholders", "-p", is_flag=True, help="Show only placeholders")
   264→@click.option("--styles", "-s", is_flag=True, help="Show available styles")
   265→def inspect(
   266→    document: str,
   267→    as_json: bool,
   268→    placeholders: bool,
   269→    styles: bool,
   270→):
   271→    """
   272→    Inspect a Word document or template.
   273→
   274→    \b
   275→    Examples:
   276→        will inspect template.docx
   277→        will inspect report.docx --json
   278→        will inspect template.docx --placeholders
   279→    """
   280→    try:
   281→        doc = WordDocument(template=document)
   282→        info = doc.get_info()
   283→
   284→        if as_json:
   285→            console.print_json(data=info)
   286→            return
   287→
   288→        if placeholders:
   289→            if info["placeholders"]:
   290→                console.print("[bold]Placeholders found:[/bold]")
   291→                for p in info["placeholders"]:
   292→                    console.print(f"  {{{{[cyan]{p}[/cyan]}}}}")
   293→            else:
   294→                console.print("[dim]No placeholders found[/dim]")
   295→            return
   296→
   297→        if styles:
   298→            console.print("[bold]Available styles:[/bold]")
   299→            for style in info["styles"]:
   300→                console.print(f"  {style}")
   301→            return
   302→
   303→        # Full inspection
   304→        console.print(Panel(f"[bold]Document: {document}[/bold]"))
   305→
   306→        table = Table(show_header=True, header_style="bold")
   307→        table.add_column("Property")
   308→        table.add_column("Value")
   309→
   310→        table.add_row("Title", info["title"] or "(none)")
   311→        table.add_row("Author", info["author"] or "(none)")
   312→        table.add_row("Created", info["created"] or "(unknown)")
   313→        table.add_row("Modified", info["modified"] or "(unknown)")
   314→        table.add_row("Paragraphs", str(info["paragraphs"]))
   315→        table.add_row("Tables", str(info["tables"]))
   316→        table.add_row("Sections", str(info["sections"]))
   317→        table.add_row("Word Count", str(info["word_count"]))
   318→
   319→        console.print(table)
   320→
   321→        if info["placeholders"]:
   322→            console.print("\n[bold]Placeholders:[/bold]")
   323→            for p in info["placeholders"]:
   324→                console.print(f"  {{{{[cyan]{p}[/cyan]}}}}")
   325→
   326→    except Exception as e:
   327→        console.print(f"[red]Error inspecting document:[/red] {e}")
   328→        sys.exit(1)
   329→
   330→
   331→# =============================================================================
   332→# NEW COMMAND (Interactive)
   333→# =============================================================================
   334→
   335→
   336→@main.command()
   337→@click.option("--output", "-o", required=True, help="Output file path (.docx)")
   338→@click.option("--template", "-t", help="Template to use")
   339→def new(output: str, template: Optional[str]):
   340→    """
   341→    Interactively create a new document.
   342→
   343→    \b
   344→    Examples:
   345→        will new -o document.docx
   346→        will new -o report.docx --template report
   347→    """
   348→    try:
   349→        console.print("[bold]Create New Document[/bold]\n")
   350→
   351→        # Get title
   352→        title = click.prompt("Document title", default="Untitled Document")
   353→
   354→        # Get subtitle
   355→        subtitle = click.prompt("Subtitle (optional)", default="", show_default=False)
   356→        subtitle = subtitle if subtitle else None
   357→
   358→        # Get author
   359→        author = click.prompt("Author", default="")
   360→        author = author if author else None
   361→
   362→        # Choose template if not specified
   363→        if not template:
   364→            console.print("\n[bold]Available templates:[/bold]")
   365→            templates = list_templates()
   366→            for i, t in enumerate(templates, 1):
   367→                console.print(f"  {i}. {t['name']} - {t['description']}")
   368→
   369→            choice = click.prompt(
   370→                "\nSelect template (number or name)", default="default", show_default=True
   371→            )
   372→
   373→            try:
   374→                idx = int(choice) - 1
   375→                if 0 <= idx < len(templates):
   376→                    template = templates[idx]["name"]
   377→            except ValueError:
   378→                template = choice
   379→
   380→        # Create document
   381→        builder = create_document(title=title, subtitle=subtitle, author=author)
   382→
   383→        # Apply template settings
   384→        if template:
   385→            builder.with_template(template)
   386→
   387→        # Interactive content addition
   388→        console.print("\n[bold]Add content (type 'done' to finish):[/bold]")
   389→
   390→        while True:
   391→            content_type = click.prompt(
   392→                "\nContent type",
   393→                type=click.Choice(["heading", "paragraph", "bullets", "table", "done"]),
   394→                default="done",
   395→            )
   396→
   397→            if content_type == "done":
   398→                break
   399→
   400→            if content_type == "heading":
   401→                text = click.prompt("Heading text")
   402→                level = click.prompt("Level (1-5)", type=int, default=1)
   403→                builder.add_heading(text, level=level)
   404→
   405→            elif content_type == "paragraph":
   406→                text = click.prompt("Paragraph text")
   407→                builder.add_paragraph(text)
   408→
   409→            elif content_type == "bullets":
   410→                console.print("Enter bullet points (empty line to finish):")
   411→                items = []
   412→                while True:
   413→                    item = click.prompt("  -", default="", show_default=False)
   414→                    if not item:
   415→                        break
   416→                    items.append(item)
   417→                if items:
   418→                    builder.add_bullets(items)
   419→
   420→            elif content_type == "table":
   421→                cols = click.prompt("Number of columns", type=int, default=2)
   422→                headers = []
   423→                console.print("Enter column headers:")
   424→                for i in range(cols):
   425→                    h = click.prompt(f"  Column {i+1}")
   426→                    headers.append(h)
   427→
   428→                rows = click.prompt("Number of rows", type=int, default=2)
   429→                data = []
   430→                console.print("Enter table data:")
   431→                for r in range(rows):
   432→                    row = []
   433→                    for c in range(cols):
   434→                        cell = click.prompt(f"  Row {r+1}, Col {c+1}")
   435→                        row.append(cell)
   436→                    data.append(row)
   437→
   438→                builder.add_table(data, headers=headers)
   439→
   440→        # Save
   441→        builder.save(output)
   442→        console.print(f"\n[green]Created document:[/green] {output}")
   443→
   444→    except click.Abort:
   445→        console.print("\n[yellow]Cancelled[/yellow]")
   446→        sys.exit(0)
   447→    except Exception as e:
   448→        console.print(f"[red]Error:[/red] {e}")
   449→        sys.exit(1)

   457→@main.command()
   458→@click.argument("document", type=click.Path(exists=True))
   459→@click.option("--output", "-o", help="Output file (defaults to overwrite input)")
   460→@click.option("--heading", "-h", help="Add a heading")
   461→@click.option("--level", "-l", type=int, default=1, help="Heading level (1-5)")

   463→@click.option("--bullets", "-b", multiple=True, help="Add bullet points")
   464→@click.option("--page-break", is_flag=True, help="Add a page break")

   474→    page_break: bool,
   475→    image: Optional[str],
   476→    caption: Optional[str],
   477→):
   478→    """
   479→    Add content to an existing document.
   480→
   481→    \b
   482→    Examples:
   483→        will add report.docx --heading "New Section" --level 1
   484→        will add report.docx --paragraph "Additional text here."
   485→        will add report.docx -b "Point 1" -b "Point 2" -b "Point 3"
   486→        will add report.docx --image chart.png --caption "Figure 1"
   487→    """
   488→    try:
   489→        doc = WordDocument(template=document)
   490→        output_path = output or document
   491→
   492→        if page_break:
   493→            doc.add_page_break()

   496→            doc.add_heading(heading, level=level)
   497→
   498→        if paragraph:
   499→            doc.add_paragraph(paragraph)
   500→
   501→        if bullets:
   502→            doc.add_bullets(list(bullets))
   503→
   504→        if image:
   505→            doc.add_image(image, caption=caption)
   506→
   507→        doc.save(output_path)
   508→        console.print(f"[green]Updated document:[/green] {output_path}")
   509→
   510→    except Exception as e:
   511→        console.print(f"[red]Error:[/red] {e}")

   515→# =============================================================================
   516→# REPLACE COMMAND
   517→# =============================================================================
   518→
   519→
   520→@main.command()
   521→@click.argument("document", type=click.Path(exists=True))
   522→@click.argument("replacements", nargs=-1)
   523→@click.option("--output", "-o", help="Output file (defaults to overwrite input)")
   524→@click.option("--list", "-l", "list_only", is_flag=True, help="List placeholders only")
   525→def replace(
   526→    document: str,
   527→    replacements: tuple,
   528→    output: Optional[str],
   529→    list_only: bool,
   530→):
   531→    """
   532→    Replace {{PLACEHOLDERS}} in a document.
   533→
   534→    \b
   535→    Examples:
   536→        will replace template.docx NAME="John Doe" DATE="2024-01-15"
   537→        will replace template.docx --list
   538→        will replace template.docx COMPANY="Acme Corp" -o output.docx
   539→    """
   540→    try:
   541→        doc = WordDocument(template=document)
   542→
   543→        if list_only:
   544→            placeholders = doc.get_placeholders()
   545→            if placeholders:
   546→                console.print("[bold]Placeholders found:[/bold]")
   547→                for p in placeholders:
   548→                    console.print(f"  {{{{[cyan]{p}[/cyan]}}}}")
   549→            else:
   550→                console.print("[dim]No placeholders found[/dim]")
   551→            return
   552→
   553→        if not replacements:
   554→            console.print("[yellow]No replacements specified. Use KEY=VALUE format.[/yellow]")
   555→            console.print("Use --list to see available placeholders.")
   556→            return
   557→
   558→        # Parse replacements
   559→        repl_dict = {}
   560→        for r in replacements:
   561→            if "=" in r:
   562→                key, value = r.split("=", 1)
   563→                repl_dict[key] = value
   564→            else:
   565→                console.print(f"[yellow]Skipping invalid format: {r} (use KEY=VALUE)[/yellow]")
   566→
   567→        if repl_dict:
   568→            count = doc.replace_placeholders(repl_dict)
   569→            output_path = output or document
   570→            doc.save(output_path)
   571→            console.print(f"[green]Replaced {count} placeholder(s) in:[/green] {output_path}")
   572→        else:
   573→            console.print("[yellow]No valid replacements to make[/yellow]")
   574→
   575→    except Exception as e:
   576→        console.print(f"[red]Error:[/red] {e}")
   577→        sys.exit(1)
   578→

   673→@main.group()
   674→def cloud():
   675→    """
   676→    Cloud API commands for remote document generation.
   677→

   686→
   687→@cloud.command("health")
   688→@click.option("--url", "-u", default="http://localhost:8000", help="API base URL")
   689→def cloud_health(url: str):
   690→    """Check cloud API health status."""
   691→    try:
   692→        import httpx
   693→
   694→        with httpx.Client() as client:
   695→            response = client.get(f"{url}/health", timeout=10)
   696→            response.raise_for_status()
   697→            data = response.json()
   698→
   699→        console.print("[green]API is healthy[/green]")
   700→        table = Table(show_header=False, box=None)
   701→        for key, value in data.items():
   702→            table.add_row(key, str(value))
   703→        console.print(table)
   704→
   705→    except Exception as e:
   706→        console.print(f"[red]API health check failed:[/red] {e}")
   707→        sys.exit(1)
   708→
   709→
   710→@cloud.command("generate")
   711→@click.argument("spec_file", type=click.Path(exists=True))
   712→@click.option("--output", "-o", required=True, help="Output file path")
   713→@click.option("--url", "-u", default="http://localhost:8000", help="API base URL")
   714→@click.option("--template", "-t", type=click.Path(exists=True), help="Template file")
   715→def cloud_generate(
   716→    spec_file: str,
   717→    output: str,
   718→    url: str,
   719→    template: Optional[str],
   720→):
   721→    """Generate document via cloud API."""
   722→    try:
   723→        import httpx
   724→        import yaml
   725→
   726→        # Load spec
   727→        spec_path = Path(spec_file)
   728→        if spec_path.suffix.lower() in [".yaml", ".yml"]:
   729→            with open(spec_path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
   730→                spec = yaml.safe_load(f)
   731→        else:
   732→            with open(spec_path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
   733→                spec = json.load(f)
   734→
   735→        with Progress(
   736→            SpinnerColumn(),
   737→            TextColumn("[progress.description]{task.description}"),
   738→            console=console,
   739→        ) as progress:
   740→            task = progress.add_task("Generating via cloud API...", total=None)
   741→
   742→            with httpx.Client() as client:
   743→                if template:
   744→                    # Use multipart form data with template
   745→                    with open(template, "rb") as f:
   746→                        files = {"template": f}
   747→                        data = {"spec": json.dumps(spec)}
   748→                        response = client.post(
   749→                            f"{url}/generate-with-template",
   750→                            files=files,
   751→                            data=data,
   752→                            timeout=60,
   753→                        )
   754→                else:
   755→                    # JSON only
   756→                    response = client.post(
   757→                        f"{url}/generate",
   758→                        json=spec,
   759→                        timeout=60,
   760→                    )
   761→
   762→                response.raise_for_status()
   763→
   764→            # Save response
   765→            with open(output, "wb") as f:
   766→                f.write(response.content)
   767→
   768→            progress.update(task, completed=True)
   769→
   770→        console.print(f"[green]Generated document:[/green] {output}")
   771→
   772→    except Exception as e:
   773→        console.print(f"[red]Cloud generation failed:[/red] {e}")
   774→        sys.exit(1)
   775→
   776→
   777→@cloud.command("inspect")
   778→@click.argument("template", type=click.Path(exists=True))
   779→@click.option("--url", "-u", default="http://localhost:8000", help="API base URL")
   780→def cloud_inspect(template: str, url: str):
   781→    """Inspect template via cloud API."""
   782→    try:
   783→        import httpx
   784→
   785→        with httpx.Client() as client:
   786→            with open(template, "rb") as f:
   787→                files = {"template": f}
   788→                response = client.post(
   789→                    f"{url}/inspect",
   790→                    files=files,
   791→                    timeout=30,
   792→                )
   793→            response.raise_for_status()
   794→            data = response.json()
   795→
   796→        console.print(Panel(f"[bold]Template: {template}[/bold]"))
   797→        console.print_json(data=data)
   798→
   799→    except Exception as e:
   800→        console.print(f"[red]Cloud inspection failed:[/red] {e}")
   801→        sys.exit(1)

     1→"""
     2→Schopenhauer Core - The WordDocument class.
     3→
     4→This module provides the main WordDocument class for creating
     5→and manipulating Word documents programmatically.
     6→
     7→Example:
     8→    >>> from will import WordDocument
     9→    >>> doc = WordDocument()
    10→    >>> doc.add_heading("My Report", level=1)
    11→    >>> doc.add_paragraph("Introduction text here.")
    12→    >>> doc.add_bullets(["Point 1", "Point 2", "Point 3"])
    13→    >>> doc.save("report.docx")
    14→"""
    15→
    16→from io import BytesIO
    17→from pathlib import Path
    18→from typing import Any, Optional, Union
    19→
    20→from docx import Document
    21→from docx.enum.section import WD_ORIENT
    22→from docx.enum.style import WD_STYLE_TYPE
    23→from docx.enum.table import WD_TABLE_ALIGNMENT
    24→from docx.enum.text import WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH
    25→from docx.oxml import parse_xml
    26→from docx.oxml.ns import nsdecls
    27→from docx.shared import Inches, Pt
    28→
    29→from will.styles import (
    30→    FONTS,
    31→    MARGINS,
    32→    PAGE_SIZES,
    33→    SPACING,
    34→    Colors,
    35→    TableStyles,
    36→)
    37→
    38→
    39→class WordDocument:
    40→    """
    41→    A high-level interface for creating Word documents.
    42→
    43→    The WordDocument class wraps python-docx to provide a simpler,
    44→    more intuitive API for common document operations.
    45→
    46→    Attributes:
    47→        doc: The underlying python-docx Document object.
    48→
    49→    Example:
    50→        >>> doc = WordDocument()
    51→        >>> doc.add_heading("Report Title", level=1)
    52→        >>> doc.add_paragraph("This is the introduction.")
    53→        >>> doc.save("report.docx")
    54→    """
    55→
    56→    def __init__(
    57→        self,
    58→        template: Optional[str] = None,
    59→        title: Optional[str] = None,
    60→        author: Optional[str] = None,
    61→    ):
    62→        """
    63→        Initialize a new WordDocument.
    64→
    65→        Args:
    66→            template: Path to a .docx template file, or None for blank.
    67→            title: Document title (stored in metadata).
    68→            author: Document author (stored in metadata).
    69→        """
    70→        if template:
    71→            template_path = Path(template)
    72→            if not template_path.exists():
    73→                raise FileNotFoundError(f"Template not found: {template}")
    74→            self.doc = Document(template_path)
    75→        else:
    76→            self.doc = Document()
    77→
    78→        # Set document properties
    79→        if title:
    80→            self.doc.core_properties.title = title
    81→        if author:
    82→            self.doc.core_properties.author = author
    83→
    84→        # Apply default styling
    85→        self._setup_default_styles()
    86→
    87→    def _setup_default_styles(self):
    88→        """Configure default styles for the document."""
    89→        # Set default font for normal style
    90→        style = self.doc.styles["Normal"]
    91→        font = style.font
    92→        font.name = FONTS.BODY.family
    93→        font.size = FONTS.BODY.size_pt
    94→        font.color.rgb = FONTS.BODY.color_rgb
    95→
    96→        # Set default paragraph formatting
    97→        para_format = style.paragraph_format
    98→        para_format.space_after = SPACING.PARA_AFTER
    99→        para_format.line_spacing = SPACING.LINE_DEFAULT
   100→
   101→    # =========================================================================
   102→    # HEADING METHODS
   103→    # =========================================================================
   104→
   105→    def add_heading(
   106→        self,
   107→        text: str,
   108→        level: int = 1,
   109→        color: Optional[str] = None,
   110→        alignment: WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.LEFT,
   111→    ):
   112→        """
   113→        Add a heading to the document.
   114→
   115→        Args:
   116→            text: The heading text.
   117→            level: Heading level (0=Title, 1-9=Heading levels).
   118→            color: Optional hex color override.
   119→            alignment: Text alignment.
   120→
   121→        Returns:
   122→            The created paragraph object.
   123→        """
   124→        heading = self.doc.add_heading(text, level=level)
   125→
   126→        # Apply styling based on level
   127→        if level == 0:
   128→            font_spec = FONTS.TITLE
   129→        elif level == 1:
   130→            font_spec = FONTS.HEADING_1
   131→        elif level == 2:
   132→            font_spec = FONTS.HEADING_2
   133→        elif level == 3:
   134→            font_spec = FONTS.HEADING_3
   135→        else:
   136→            font_spec = FONTS.HEADING_4
   137→
   138→        for run in heading.runs:
   139→            run.font.name = font_spec.family
   140→            run.font.size = font_spec.size_pt
   141→            run.font.bold = font_spec.bold
   142→            if color:
   143→                run.font.color.rgb = Colors.from_hex(color)
   144→            else:
   145→                run.font.color.rgb = font_spec.color_rgb
   146→
   147→        heading.alignment = alignment
   148→        return heading
   149→
   150→    def add_title(
   151→        self,
   152→        text: str,
   153→        subtitle: Optional[str] = None,
   154→        centered: bool = True,
   155→    ):
   156→        """
   157→        Add a document title (and optional subtitle).
   158→
   159→        Args:
   160→            text: The main title text.
   161→            subtitle: Optional subtitle text.
   162→            centered: Whether to center the title.
   163→
   164→        Returns:
   165→            Tuple of (title_paragraph, subtitle_paragraph or None)
   166→        """
   167→        alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER if centered else WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.LEFT
   168→
   169→        title = self.add_heading(text, level=0, alignment=alignment)
   170→
   171→        subtitle_para = None
   172→        if subtitle:
   173→            subtitle_para = self.doc.add_paragraph()
   174→            run = subtitle_para.add_run(subtitle)
   175→            run.font.name = FONTS.SUBTITLE.family
   176→            run.font.size = FONTS.SUBTITLE.size_pt
   177→            run.font.italic = FONTS.SUBTITLE.italic
   178→            run.font.color.rgb = FONTS.SUBTITLE.color_rgb
   179→            subtitle_para.alignment = alignment
   180→
   181→        return title, subtitle_para
   182→
   183→    # =========================================================================
   184→    # PARAGRAPH METHODS
   185→    # =========================================================================
   186→
   187→    def add_paragraph(
   188→        self,
   189→        text: str = "",
   190→        style: Optional[str] = None,
   191→        bold: bool = False,
   192→        italic: bool = False,
   193→        font_size: Optional[int] = None,
   194→        font_name: Optional[str] = None,
   195→        color: Optional[str] = None,
   196→        alignment: Optional[WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH] = None,
   197→        first_line_indent: Optional[float] = None,
   198→        space_before: Optional[int] = None,
   199→        space_after: Optional[int] = None,
   200→    ):
   201→        """
   202→        Add a paragraph to the document.
   203→
   204→        Args:
   205→            text: The paragraph text.
   206→            style: Built-in style name to apply.
   207→            bold: Whether text should be bold.
   208→            italic: Whether text should be italic.
   209→            font_size: Font size in points.
   210→            font_name: Font family name.
   211→            color: Hex color for the text.
   212→            alignment: Text alignment.
   213→            first_line_indent: First line indent in inches.
   214→            space_before: Space before paragraph in points.
   215→            space_after: Space after paragraph in points.
   216→
   217→        Returns:
   218→            The created paragraph object.
   219→        """
   220→        para = self.doc.add_paragraph(style=style)
   221→
   222→        if text:
   223→            run = para.add_run(text)
   224→            run.bold = bold
   225→            run.italic = italic
   226→
   227→            if font_size:
   228→                run.font.size = Pt(font_size)
   229→            if font_name:
   230→                run.font.name = font_name
   231→            if color:
   232→                run.font.color.rgb = Colors.from_hex(color)
   233→
   234→        if alignment:
   235→            para.alignment = alignment
   236→
   237→        para_format = para.paragraph_format
   238→        if first_line_indent is not None:
   239→            para_format.first_line_indent = Inches(first_line_indent)
   240→        if space_before is not None:
   241→            para_format.space_before = Pt(space_before)
   242→        if space_after is not None:
   243→            para_format.space_after = Pt(space_after)
   244→
   245→        return para
   246→
   247→    def add_text(
   248→        self,
   249→        paragraph,
   250→        text: str,
   251→        bold: bool = False,
   252→        italic: bool = False,
   253→        underline: bool = False,
   254→        font_size: Optional[int] = None,
   255→        font_name: Optional[str] = None,
   256→        color: Optional[str] = None,
   257→    ):
   258→        """
   259→        Add formatted text to an existing paragraph.
   260→
   261→        Args:
   262→            paragraph: The paragraph to add text to.
   263→            text: The text to add.
   264→            bold: Whether text should be bold.
   265→            italic: Whether text should be italic.
   266→            underline: Whether text should be underlined.
   267→            font_size: Font size in points.
   268→            font_name: Font family name.
   269→            color: Hex color for the text.
   270→
   271→        Returns:
   272→            The created run object.
   273→        """
   274→        run = paragraph.add_run(text)
   275→        run.bold = bold
   276→        run.italic = italic
   277→        run.underline = underline
   278→
   279→        if font_size:
   280→            run.font.size = Pt(font_size)
   281→        if font_name:
   282→            run.font.name = font_name
   283→        if color:
   284→            run.font.color.rgb = Colors.from_hex(color)
   285→
   286→        return run
   287→
   288→    # =========================================================================
   289→    # LIST METHODS
   290→    # =========================================================================
   291→
   292→    def add_bullets(
   293→        self,
   294→        items: list[str],
   295→        level: int = 0,
   296→        style: str = "List Bullet",
   297→    ):
   298→        """
   299→        Add a bulleted list to the document.
   300→
   301→        Args:
   302→            items: List of strings for bullet points.
   303→            level: Indentation level (0-8).
   304→            style: The bullet style to use.
   305→
   306→        Returns:
   307→            List of created paragraph objects.
   308→        """
   309→        paragraphs = []
   310→        for item in items:
   311→            para = self.doc.add_paragraph(item, style=style)
   312→            # Set indentation level
   313→            if level > 0:
   314→                para.paragraph_format.left_indent = Inches(0.25 * (level + 1))
   315→            paragraphs.append(para)
   316→        return paragraphs
   317→
   318→    def add_numbered_list(
   319→        self,
   320→        items: list[str],
   321→        level: int = 0,
   322→        style: str = "List Number",
   323→    ):
   324→        """
   325→        Add a numbered list to the document.
   326→
   327→        Args:
   328→            items: List of strings for numbered items.
   329→            level: Indentation level (0-8).
   330→            style: The numbering style to use.
   331→
   332→        Returns:
   333→            List of created paragraph objects.
   334→        """
   335→        paragraphs = []
   336→        for item in items:
   337→            para = self.doc.add_paragraph(item, style=style)
   338→            if level > 0:
   339→                para.paragraph_format.left_indent = Inches(0.25 * (level + 1))
   340→            paragraphs.append(para)
   341→        return paragraphs
   342→
   343→    # =========================================================================
   344→    # TABLE METHODS
   345→    # =========================================================================
   346→
   347→    def add_table(
   348→        self,
   349→        data: list[list[Any]],
   350→        headers: Optional[list[str]] = None,
   351→        style: str = "Table Grid",
   352→        header_color: Optional[str] = None,
   353→        alternating_rows: bool = True,
   354→        column_widths: Optional[list[float]] = None,
   355→    ):
   356→        """
   357→        Add a table to the document.
   358→
   359→        Args:
   360→            data: 2D list of cell values.
   361→            headers: Optional list of header strings.
   362→            style: Table style name.
   363→            header_color: Hex color for header background.
   364→            alternating_rows: Whether to shade alternate rows.
   365→            column_widths: List of column widths in inches.
   366→
   367→        Returns:
   368→            The created table object.
   369→        """
   370→        num_cols = len(headers) if headers else len(data[0]) if data else 0
   371→        num_rows = len(data) + (1 if headers else 0)
   372→
   373→        table = self.doc.add_table(rows=num_rows, cols=num_cols)
   374→        table.style = style
   375→        table.alignment = WD_TABLE_ALIGNMENT.CENTER
   376→
   377→        # Set column widths if provided
   378→        if column_widths:
   379→            for i, width in enumerate(column_widths):
   380→                if i < len(table.columns):
   381→                    for cell in table.columns[i].cells:
   382→                        cell.width = Inches(width)
   383→
   384→        row_offset = 0
   385→
   386→        # Add headers
   387→        if headers:
   388→            header_row = table.rows[0]
   389→            for i, header in enumerate(headers):
   390→                cell = header_row.cells[i]
   391→                cell.text = str(header)
   392→                # Apply header styling
   393→                color = Colors.from_hex(header_color) if header_color else Colors.PRIMARY
   394→                TableStyles.apply_header_style(cell, color)
   395→            row_offset = 1
   396→
   397→        # Add data rows
   398→        for row_idx, row_data in enumerate(data):
   399→            table_row = table.rows[row_idx + row_offset]
   400→            for col_idx, cell_value in enumerate(row_data):
   401→                cell = table_row.cells[col_idx]
   402→                cell.text = str(cell_value)
   403→                TableStyles.apply_body_style(cell)
   404→
   405→        # Apply alternating row colors
   406→        if alternating_rows:
   407→            TableStyles.apply_alternating_rows(table)
   408→
   409→        return table
   410→
   411→    # =========================================================================
   412→    # IMAGE METHODS
   413→    # =========================================================================
   414→
   415→    def add_image(
   416→        self,
   417→        image_path: str,
   418→        width: Optional[float] = None,
   419→        height: Optional[float] = None,
   420→        caption: Optional[str] = None,
   421→        alignment: WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER,
   422→    ):
   423→        """
   424→        Add an image to the document.
   425→
   426→        Args:
   427→            image_path: Path to the image file.
   428→            width: Image width in inches (maintains aspect ratio if height not set).
   429→            height: Image height in inches.
   430→            caption: Optional caption text below the image.
   431→            alignment: Alignment of the image paragraph.
   432→
   433→        Returns:
   434→            Tuple of (paragraph, inline_shape, caption_paragraph or None)
   435→        """
   436→        para = self.doc.add_paragraph()
   437→        para.alignment = alignment
   438→
   439→        # Determine dimensions
   440→        width_inches = Inches(width) if width else None
   441→        height_inches = Inches(height) if height else None
   442→
   443→        run = para.add_run()
   444→        picture = run.add_picture(image_path, width=width_inches, height=height_inches)
   445→
   446→        caption_para = None
   447→        if caption:
   448→            caption_para = self.doc.add_paragraph()
   449→            run = caption_para.add_run(caption)
   450→            run.font.name = FONTS.CAPTION.family
   451→            run.font.size = FONTS.CAPTION.size_pt
   452→            run.font.italic = FONTS.CAPTION.italic
   453→            run.font.color.rgb = FONTS.CAPTION.color_rgb
   454→            caption_para.alignment = alignment
   455→
   456→        return para, picture, caption_para
   457→
   458→    # =========================================================================
   459→    # SPECIAL CONTENT METHODS
   460→    # =========================================================================
   461→
   462→    def add_quote(
   463→        self,
   464→        text: str,
   465→        author: Optional[str] = None,
   466→        style: str = "Quote",
   467→    ):
   468→        """
   469→        Add a blockquote to the document.
   470→
   471→        Args:
   472→            text: The quote text.
   473→            author: Optional attribution.
   474→            style: The quote style to use.
   475→
   476→        Returns:
   477→            List of created paragraphs.
   478→        """
   479→        paragraphs = []
   480→
   481→        # Add the quote
   482→        quote_para = self.doc.add_paragraph(style=style)
   483→        run = quote_para.add_run(f'"{text}"')
   484→        run.font.name = FONTS.QUOTE.family
   485→        run.font.size = FONTS.QUOTE.size_pt
   486→        run.font.italic = True
   487→        run.font.color.rgb = FONTS.QUOTE.color_rgb
   488→        quote_para.paragraph_format.left_indent = Inches(0.5)
   489→        quote_para.paragraph_format.right_indent = Inches(0.5)
   490→        paragraphs.append(quote_para)
   491→
   492→        # Add attribution
   493→        if author:
   494→            attr_para = self.doc.add_paragraph()
   495→            run = attr_para.add_run(f"— {author}")
   496→            run.font.name = FONTS.CAPTION.family
   497→            run.font.size = FONTS.CAPTION.size_pt
   498→            run.font.italic = True
   499→            run.font.color.rgb = FONTS.CAPTION.color_rgb
   500→            attr_para.alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.RIGHT
   501→            attr_para.paragraph_format.right_indent = Inches(0.5)
   502→            paragraphs.append(attr_para)
   503→
   504→        return paragraphs
   505→
   506→    def add_code_block(
   507→        self,
   508→        code: str,
   509→        language: Optional[str] = None,
   510→    ):
   511→        """
   512→        Add a code block to the document.
   513→
   514→        Args:
   515→            code: The code text.
   516→            language: Optional language identifier (for documentation).
   517→
   518→        Returns:
   519→            The created paragraph.
   520→        """
   521→        para = self.doc.add_paragraph()
   522→        run = para.add_run(code)
   523→        run.font.name = FONTS.CODE.family
   524→        run.font.size = FONTS.CODE.size_pt
   525→        run.font.color.rgb = FONTS.CODE.color_rgb
   526→
   527→        # Add light gray background
   528→        para.paragraph_format.space_before = Pt(6)
   529→        para.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(6)
   530→        para.paragraph_format.left_indent = Inches(0.25)
   531→
   532→        return para
   533→
   534→    def add_horizontal_line(self):
   535→        """Add a horizontal line (border) to the document."""
   536→        para = self.doc.add_paragraph()
   537→        # Add bottom border to simulate horizontal line
   538→        pBdr = parse_xml(
   539→            f'<w:pBdr {nsdecls("w")}>'
   540→            f'<w:bottom w:val="single" w:sz="6" w:space="1" w:color="{Colors.GRAY_400.value}"/>'
   541→            f"</w:pBdr>"
   542→        )
   543→        para._p.get_or_add_pPr().append(pBdr)
   544→        return para
   545→
   546→    def add_page_break(self):
   547→        """Add a page break to the document."""
   548→        self.doc.add_page_break()
   549→
   550→    def add_section_break(self, start_type=None):
   551→        """Add a section break to the document."""
   552→        self.doc.add_section(start_type)
   553→
   554→    # =========================================================================
   555→    # TABLE OF CONTENTS
   556→    # =========================================================================
   557→
   558→    def add_table_of_contents(
   559→        self,
   560→        title: str = "Table of Contents",
   561→        levels: int = 3,
   562→    ):
   563→        """
   564→        Add a table of contents field to the document.
   565→
   566→        Note: The TOC will need to be updated when opened in Word.
   567→
   568→        Args:
   569→            title: Title for the TOC section.
   570→            levels: Number of heading levels to include.
   571→
   572→        Returns:
   573→            The TOC paragraph.
   574→        """
   575→        # Add TOC title
   576→        self.add_heading(title, level=1)
   577→
   578→        # Add TOC field
   579→        para = self.doc.add_paragraph()
   580→
   581→        # Create TOC field code
   582→        run = para.add_run()
   583→        fldChar1 = parse_xml(f'<w:fldChar {nsdecls("w")} w:fldCharType="begin"/>')
   584→        run._r.append(fldChar1)
   585→
   586→        run2 = para.add_run()
   587→        instrText = parse_xml(
   588→            f'<w:instrText {nsdecls("w")} xml:space="preserve"> TOC \\o "1-{levels}" \\h \\z \\u </w:instrText>'
   589→        )
   590→        run2._r.append(instrText)
   591→
   592→        run3 = para.add_run()
   593→        fldChar2 = parse_xml(f'<w:fldChar {nsdecls("w")} w:fldCharType="separate"/>')
   594→        run3._r.append(fldChar2)
   595→
   596→        run4 = para.add_run("Right-click and select 'Update Field' to generate TOC")
   597→        run4.font.color.rgb = Colors.GRAY_500.rgb
   598→        run4.italic = True
   599→
   600→        run5 = para.add_run()
   601→        fldChar3 = parse_xml(f'<w:fldChar {nsdecls("w")} w:fldCharType="end"/>')
   602→        run5._r.append(fldChar3)
   603→
   604→        # Add page break after TOC
   605→        self.add_page_break()
   606→
   607→        return para
   608→
   609→    # =========================================================================
   610→    # HEADER/FOOTER METHODS
   611→    # =========================================================================
   612→
   613→    def set_header(
   614→        self,
   615→        text: str,
   616→        alignment: WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER,
   617→    ):
   618→        """
   619→        Set the document header text.
   620→
   621→        Args:
   622→            text: Header text.
   623→            alignment: Text alignment.
   624→
   625→        Returns:
   626→            The header paragraph.
   627→        """
   628→        section = self.doc.sections[0]
   629→        header = section.header
   630→        para = header.paragraphs[0] if header.paragraphs else header.add_paragraph()
   631→        para.clear()
   632→
   633→        run = para.add_run(text)
   634→        run.font.name = FONTS.HEADER.family
   635→        run.font.size = FONTS.HEADER.size_pt
   636→        run.font.color.rgb = FONTS.HEADER.color_rgb
   637→        para.alignment = alignment
   638→
   639→        return para
   640→
   641→    def set_footer(
   642→        self,
   643→        text: str = "",
   644→        include_page_numbers: bool = True,
   645→        alignment: WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER,
   646→    ):
   647→        """
   648→        Set the document footer.
   649→
   650→        Args:
   651→            text: Footer text.
   652→            include_page_numbers: Whether to include page numbers.
   653→            alignment: Text alignment.
   654→
   655→        Returns:
   656→            The footer paragraph.
   657→        """
   658→        section = self.doc.sections[0]
   659→        footer = section.footer
   660→        para = footer.paragraphs[0] if footer.paragraphs else footer.add_paragraph()
   661→        para.clear()
   662→
   663→        if text:
   664→            run = para.add_run(text)
   665→            run.font.name = FONTS.FOOTER.family
   666→            run.font.size = FONTS.FOOTER.size_pt
   667→            run.font.color.rgb = FONTS.FOOTER.color_rgb
   668→
   669→        if include_page_numbers:
   670→            if text:
   671→                para.add_run(" | ")
   672→
   673→            # Add page number field
   674→            run = para.add_run()
   675→            fldChar1 = parse_xml(f'<w:fldChar {nsdecls("w")} w:fldCharType="begin"/>')
   676→            run._r.append(fldChar1)
   677→
   678→            run2 = para.add_run()
   679→            instrText = parse_xml(
   680→                f'<w:instrText {nsdecls("w")} xml:space="preserve"> PAGE </w:instrText>'
   681→            )
   682→            run2._r.append(instrText)
   683→
   684→            run3 = para.add_run()
   685→            fldChar2 = parse_xml(f'<w:fldChar {nsdecls("w")} w:fldCharType="end"/>')
   686→            run3._r.append(fldChar2)
   687→
   688→        para.alignment = alignment
   689→        return para
   690→
   691→    # =========================================================================
   692→    # PAGE SETUP METHODS
   693→    # =========================================================================
   694→
   695→    def set_margins(
   696→        self,
   697→        top: Optional[float] = None,
   698→        bottom: Optional[float] = None,
   699→        left: Optional[float] = None,
   700→        right: Optional[float] = None,
   701→        preset: str = None,
   702→    ):
   703→        """
   704→        Set page margins.
   705→
   706→        Args:
   707→            top: Top margin in inches.
   708→            bottom: Bottom margin in inches.
   709→            left: Left margin in inches.
   710→            right: Right margin in inches.
   711→            preset: Use a preset ("normal", "narrow", "moderate", "wide").
   712→        """
   713→        section = self.doc.sections[0]
   714→
   715→        if preset:
   716→            presets = {
   717→                "normal": MARGINS.NORMAL,
   718→                "narrow": MARGINS.NARROW,
   719→                "moderate": MARGINS.MODERATE,
   720→                "wide": MARGINS.WIDE,
   721→            }
   722→            margins = presets.get(preset.lower(), MARGINS.NORMAL)
   723→            section.top_margin = margins["top"]
   724→            section.bottom_margin = margins["bottom"]
   725→            section.left_margin = margins["left"]
   726→            section.right_margin = margins["right"]
   727→        else:
   728→            if top is not None:
   729→                section.top_margin = Inches(top)
   730→            if bottom is not None:
   731→                section.bottom_margin = Inches(bottom)
   732→            if left is not None:
   733→                section.left_margin = Inches(left)
   734→            if right is not None:
   735→                section.right_margin = Inches(right)
   736→
   737→    def set_page_size(
   738→        self,
   739→        width: Optional[float] = None,
   740→        height: Optional[float] = None,
   741→        preset: str = None,
   742→        orientation: str = "portrait",
   743→    ):
   744→        """
   745→        Set page size.
   746→
   747→        Args:
   748→            width: Page width in inches.
   749→            height: Page height in inches.
   750→            preset: Use a preset ("letter", "legal", "a4", "a5").
   751→            orientation: "portrait" or "landscape".
   752→        """
   753→        section = self.doc.sections[0]
   754→
   755→        if preset:
   756→            presets = {
   757→                "letter": PAGE_SIZES.LETTER,
   758→                "legal": PAGE_SIZES.LEGAL,
   759→                "a4": PAGE_SIZES.A4,
   760→                "a5": PAGE_SIZES.A5,
   761→            }
   762→            size = presets.get(preset.lower(), PAGE_SIZES.LETTER)
   763→            section.page_width = size["width"]
   764→            section.page_height = size["height"]
   765→        else:
   766→            if width is not None:
   767→                section.page_width = Inches(width)
   768→            if height is not None:
   769→                section.page_height = Inches(height)
   770→
   771→        if orientation.lower() == "landscape":
   772→            section.orientation = WD_ORIENT.LANDSCAPE
   773→            # Swap width and height for landscape
   774→            section.page_width, section.page_height = section.page_height, section.page_width
   775→
   776→    # =========================================================================
   777→    # TEMPLATE/PLACEHOLDER METHODS
   778→    # =========================================================================
   779→
   780→    def replace_placeholders(self, replacements: dict[str, str]):
   781→        """
   782→        Replace {{PLACEHOLDER}} tokens throughout the document.
   783→
   784→        Args:
   785→            replacements: Dictionary of placeholder names to values.
   786→                         Example: {"NAME": "John", "DATE": "2024-01-15"}
   787→
   788→        Returns:
   789→            Number of replacements made.
   790→        """
   791→        count = 0
   792→
   793→        # Replace in paragraphs
   794→        for para in self.doc.paragraphs:
   795→            for key, value in replacements.items():
   796→                placeholder = f"{{{{{key}}}}}"
   797→                if placeholder in para.text:
   798→                    # Need to handle runs carefully
   799→                    for run in para.runs:
   800→                        if placeholder in run.text:
   801→                            run.text = run.text.replace(placeholder, str(value))
   802→                            count += 1
   803→
   804→        # Replace in tables
   805→        for table in self.doc.tables:
   806→            for row in table.rows:
   807→                for cell in row.cells:
   808→                    for para in cell.paragraphs:
   809→                        for key, value in replacements.items():
   810→                            placeholder = f"{{{{{key}}}}}"
   811→                            for run in para.runs:
   812→                                if placeholder in run.text:
   813→                                    run.text = run.text.replace(placeholder, str(value))
   814→                                    count += 1
   815→
   816→        # Replace in headers/footers
   817→        for section in self.doc.sections:
   818→            for para in section.header.paragraphs:
   819→                for key, value in replacements.items():
   820→                    placeholder = f"{{{{{key}}}}}"
   821→                    for run in para.runs:
   822→                        if placeholder in run.text:
   823→                            run.text = run.text.replace(placeholder, str(value))
   824→                            count += 1
   825→
   826→            for para in section.footer.paragraphs:
   827→                for key, value in replacements.items():
   828→                    placeholder = f"{{{{{key}}}}}"
   829→                    for run in para.runs:
   830→                        if placeholder in run.text:
   831→                            run.text = run.text.replace(placeholder, str(value))
   832→                            count += 1
   833→
   834→        return count
   835→
   836→    def get_placeholders(self) -> list[str]:
   837→        """
   838→        Find all {{PLACEHOLDER}} tokens in the document.
   839→
   840→        Returns:
   841→            List of placeholder names found.
   842→        """
   843→        import re
   844→
   845→        placeholders = set()
   846→        pattern = r"\{\{([^}]+)\}\}"
   847→
   848→        # Check paragraphs
   849→        for para in self.doc.paragraphs:
   850→            matches = re.findall(pattern, para.text)
   851→            placeholders.update(matches)
   852→
   853→        # Check tables
   854→        for table in self.doc.tables:
   855→            for row in table.rows:
   856→                for cell in row.cells:
   857→                    for para in cell.paragraphs:
   858→                        matches = re.findall(pattern, para.text)
   859→                        placeholders.update(matches)
   860→
   861→        # Check headers/footers
   862→        for section in self.doc.sections:
   863→            for para in section.header.paragraphs:
   864→                matches = re.findall(pattern, para.text)
   865→                placeholders.update(matches)
   866→            for para in section.footer.paragraphs:
   867→                matches = re.findall(pattern, para.text)
   868→                placeholders.update(matches)
   869→
   870→        return sorted(placeholders)
   871→
   872→    # =========================================================================
   873→    # DOCUMENT INFO METHODS
   874→    # =========================================================================
   875→
   876→    def get_info(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
   877→        """
   878→        Get document metadata and statistics.
   879→
   880→        Returns:
   881→            Dictionary containing document information.
   882→        """
   883→        info = {
   884→            "title": self.doc.core_properties.title or "",
   885→            "author": self.doc.core_properties.author or "",
   886→            "created": (
   887→                str(self.doc.core_properties.created) if self.doc.core_properties.created else ""
   888→            ),
   889→            "modified": (
   890→                str(self.doc.core_properties.modified) if self.doc.core_properties.modified else ""
   891→            ),
   892→            "paragraphs": len(self.doc.paragraphs),
   893→            "tables": len(self.doc.tables),
   894→            "sections": len(self.doc.sections),
   895→            "styles": [s.name for s in self.doc.styles if s.type == WD_STYLE_TYPE.PARAGRAPH],
   896→            "placeholders": self.get_placeholders(),
   897→        }
   898→
   899→        # Word count
   900→        word_count = 0
   901→        for para in self.doc.paragraphs:
   902→            word_count += len(para.text.split())
   903→        info["word_count"] = word_count
   904→
   905→        return info
   906→
   907→    # =========================================================================
   908→    # SAVE METHODS
   909→    # =========================================================================
   910→
   911→    def save(self, path: Union[str, Path]):
   912→        """
   913→        Save the document to a file.
   914→
   915→        Args:
   916→            path: Output file path.
   917→        """
   918→        self.doc.save(str(path))
   919→
   920→    def to_bytes(self) -> bytes:
   921→        """
   922→        Get the document as bytes.
   923→
   924→        Returns:
   925→            Document content as bytes.
   926→        """
   927→        buffer = BytesIO()
   928→        self.doc.save(buffer)
   929→        buffer.seek(0)
   930→        return buffer.read()
   931→
   932→    # =========================================================================
   933→    # CLASS METHODS
   934→    # =========================================================================
   935→
   936→    @classmethod
   937→    def from_yaml(cls, yaml_path: str, output_path: Optional[str] = None) -> "WordDocument":
   938→        """
   939→        Create a document from a YAML specification.
   940→
   941→        Args:
   942→            yaml_path: Path to YAML file.
   943→            output_path: Optional path to save the document.
   944→
   945→        Returns:
   946→            The created WordDocument.
   947→        """
   948→        import yaml
   949→
   950→        with open(yaml_path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
   951→            spec = yaml.safe_load(f)
   952→
   953→        doc = cls.from_spec(spec)
   954→
   955→        if output_path:
   956→            doc.save(output_path)
   957→
   958→        return doc
   959→
   960→    @classmethod
   961→    def from_json(cls, json_path: str, output_path: Optional[str] = None) -> "WordDocument":
   962→        """
   963→        Create a document from a JSON specification.
   964→
   965→        Args:
   966→            json_path: Path to JSON file.
   967→            output_path: Optional path to save the document.
   968→
   969→        Returns:
   970→            The created WordDocument.
   971→        """
   972→        import json
   973→
   974→        with open(json_path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
   975→            spec = json.load(f)
   976→
   977→        doc = cls.from_spec(spec)
   978→
   979→        if output_path:
   980→            doc.save(output_path)
   981→
   982→        return doc
   983→
   984→    @classmethod
   985→    def from_spec(cls, spec: dict[str, Any]) -> "WordDocument":
   986→        """
   987→        Create a document from a specification dictionary.
   988→
   989→        Args:
   990→            spec: Document specification dictionary.
   991→
   992→        Returns:
   993→            The created WordDocument.
   994→        """
   995→        # Get document-level settings
   996→        template = spec.get("template")
   997→        title = spec.get("title", "")
   998→        subtitle = spec.get("subtitle")
   999→        author = spec.get("author")
  1000→        page_size = spec.get("page_size", "letter")
  1001→        margins = spec.get("margins", "normal")
  1002→        header = spec.get("header")
  1003→        footer = spec.get("footer")
  1004→        include_toc = spec.get("table_of_contents", False)
  1005→
  1006→        # Create document
  1007→        doc = cls(template=template, title=title, author=author)
  1008→
  1009→        # Page setup
  1010→        doc.set_page_size(preset=page_size)
  1011→        doc.set_margins(preset=margins)
  1012→
  1013→        # Header/footer
  1014→        if header:
  1015→            doc.set_header(header)
  1016→        if footer:
  1017→            doc.set_footer(footer)
  1018→
  1019→        # Title page
  1020→        if title:
  1021→            doc.add_title(title, subtitle)
  1022→            if spec.get("title_page_break", True):
  1023→                doc.add_page_break()
  1024→
  1025→        # Table of contents
  1026→        if include_toc:
  1027→            doc.add_table_of_contents()
  1028→
  1029→        # Process sections/content
  1030→        sections = spec.get("sections", [])
  1031→        for section in sections:
  1032→            doc._process_section(section)
  1033→
  1034→        return doc
  1035→
  1036→    def _process_section(self, section: dict[str, Any]):
  1037→        """Process a section from the spec."""
  1038→        section_type = section.get("type", "content")
  1039→
  1040→        if section_type == "heading":
  1041→            level = section.get("level", 1)
  1042→            self.add_heading(section.get("title", ""), level=level)
  1043→
  1044→        elif section_type == "section":
  1045→            # Section header with optional page break
  1046→            if section.get("page_break", False):
  1047→                self.add_page_break()
  1048→            self.add_heading(section.get("title", ""), level=1)
  1049→            if section.get("subtitle"):
  1050→                self.add_paragraph(section["subtitle"], italic=True)
  1051→
  1052→        elif section_type == "content":
  1053→            if section.get("title"):
  1054→                level = section.get("level", 2)
  1055→                self.add_heading(section["title"], level=level)
  1056→
  1057→            if section.get("text"):
  1058→                self.add_paragraph(section["text"])
  1059→
  1060→            if section.get("bullets"):
  1061→                self.add_bullets(section["bullets"])
  1062→
  1063→            if section.get("numbered"):
  1064→                self.add_numbered_list(section["numbered"])
  1065→
  1066→        elif section_type == "table":
  1067→            if section.get("title"):
  1068→                self.add_heading(section["title"], level=2)
  1069→            self.add_table(
  1070→                data=section.get("data", []),
  1071→                headers=section.get("headers"),
  1072→                column_widths=section.get("column_widths"),
  1073→            )
  1074→
  1075→        elif section_type == "image":
  1076→            self.add_image(
  1077→                image_path=section.get("path", section.get("image", "")),
  1078→                width=section.get("width"),
  1079→                height=section.get("height"),
  1080→                caption=section.get("caption", section.get("title")),
  1081→            )
  1082→
  1083→        elif section_type == "quote":
  1084→            self.add_quote(
  1085→                text=section.get("text", ""),
  1086→                author=section.get("author"),
  1087→            )
  1088→
  1089→        elif section_type == "code":
  1090→            if section.get("title"):
  1091→                self.add_heading(section["title"], level=3)
  1092→            self.add_code_block(
  1093→                code=section.get("code", section.get("text", "")),
  1094→                language=section.get("language"),
  1095→            )
  1096→
  1097→        elif section_type == "page_break":
  1098→            self.add_page_break()
  1099→
  1100→        elif section_type == "horizontal_line":
  1101→            self.add_horizontal_line()
  1102→

     1→"""
     2→Tests for the WordDocument core class.
     3→"""
     4→
     5→import os
     6→import tempfile
     7→
     8→import pytest
     9→
    10→from will.core import WordDocument
    11→
    12→
    13→class TestWordDocumentCreation:
    14→    """Tests for creating WordDocument instances."""
    15→
    16→    def test_create_empty_document(self):
    17→        """Test creating an empty document."""
    18→        doc = WordDocument()
    19→        assert doc.doc is not None
    20→
    21→    def test_create_document_with_title(self):
    22→        """Test creating a document with title."""
    23→        doc = WordDocument(title="Test Title")
    24→        assert doc.doc.core_properties.title == "Test Title"
    25→
    26→    def test_create_document_with_author(self):
    27→        """Test creating a document with author."""
    28→        doc = WordDocument(author="Test Author")
    29→        assert doc.doc.core_properties.author == "Test Author"
    30→
    31→    def test_create_document_with_nonexistent_template(self):
    32→        """Test that creating with nonexistent template raises error."""
    33→        with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError):
    34→            WordDocument(template="nonexistent.docx")
    35→
    36→
    37→class TestHeadings:
    38→    """Tests for heading functionality."""
    39→
    40→    def test_add_heading_level_1(self):
    41→        """Test adding a level 1 heading."""
    42→        doc = WordDocument()
    43→        heading = doc.add_heading("Test Heading", level=1)
    44→        assert heading is not None
    45→        assert "Test Heading" in heading.text
    46→
    47→    def test_add_heading_level_2(self):
    48→        """Test adding a level 2 heading."""
    49→        doc = WordDocument()
    50→        heading = doc.add_heading("Subheading", level=2)
    51→        assert "Subheading" in heading.text
    52→
    53→    def test_add_title(self):
    54→        """Test adding title with subtitle."""
    55→        doc = WordDocument()
    56→        title, subtitle = doc.add_title("Main Title", "Subtitle Text")
    57→        assert "Main Title" in title.text
    58→        assert subtitle is not None
    59→        assert "Subtitle Text" in subtitle.text
    60→
    61→    def test_add_title_without_subtitle(self):
    62→        """Test adding title without subtitle."""

"""Preprocessing engine for Schopenhauer.

Handles variable substitution and template directives in Markdown content
before passing to Pandoc. Uses Jinja2 under the hood with permissive
undefined handling so unresolved variables pass through unchanged.
"""

import logging

from jinja2 import BaseLoader, Environment, Undefined

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class PreprocessError(Exception):
    """Raised when preprocessing fails."""


class _PassthroughUndefined(Undefined):
    """Jinja2 Undefined subclass that renders back to ``{{name}}``."""

    def __str__(self) -> str:
        return "{{" + self._undefined_name + "}}"

    def __iter__(self):
        return iter([])

    def __bool__(self) -> bool:
        return False


def preprocess(content: str, variables: dict | None = None) -> str:
    """Preprocess Markdown content with variable substitution.

    Replaces ``{{var}}`` tokens in *content* with values from *variables*.
    Unresolved tokens are left as-is (passthrough) for backward compatibility.

    Args:
        content: Raw Markdown string (may contain ``{{var}}`` tokens).
        variables: Mapping of variable names to replacement values.

    Returns:
        The processed Markdown string.

    Raises:
        PreprocessError: If Jinja2 rendering fails for a reason other than
            missing variables (e.g. syntax error in a directive).
    """
    if variables is None:
        variables = {}

    # Use Jinja2 with {{ }} delimiters (the default) and passthrough undefined
    env = Environment(
        loader=BaseLoader(),
        undefined=_PassthroughUndefined,
        keep_trailing_newline=True,
    )

    try:
        template = env.from_string(content)
        return template.render(**variables)
    except Exception as exc:
        raise PreprocessError(f"Preprocessing failed: {exc}") from exc
